{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_9","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"python - <<'PY'\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom math import radians, sin, cos, sqrt, atan2\n\n# Load data\nstations = pd.read_csv('/app/dataset/stations.csv').set_index('station_id')\nreadings = pd.read_csv('/app/dataset/readings.csv', parse_dates=['timestamp'])\nweather_stations = pd.read_csv('/app/dataset/weather_stations.csv').set_index('weather_station_id')\nweather = pd.read_csv('/app/dataset/weather_readings.csv', parse_dates=['timestamp'])\n\nstation_pollutant = readings.groupby('station_id')['pollutant'].agg(lambda x: x.mode().iat[0])\nstations = stations.join(station_pollutant.rename('pollutant'))\n\n# haversine in km\nR = 6371.0\ndef hav(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2):\n    lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2 = map(radians, [lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2])\n    dlat = lat2-lat1; dlon = lon2-lon1\n    a = sin(dlat/2)**2 + cos(lat1)*cos(lat2)*sin(dlon/2)**2\n    return 2*R*atan2(sqrt(a), sqrt(1-a))\n\n# nearest weather station mapping\nws_map = {}\nfor sid, row in stations.iterrows():\n    dists = weather_stations.apply(lambda r: hav(row.latitude,row.longitude,r.latitude,r.longitude), axis=1)\n    ws_map[sid] = dists.idxmin()\n\nweather_num = weather.drop(columns=['district']).set_index(['weather_station_id','timestamp']).sort_index()\n# standardized weather per station via nearest ws\nstation_weather = {}\nfor sid, wsid in ws_map.items():\n    w = weather_num.loc[wsid].copy()\n    station_weather[sid] = w\n\n# per pollutant pivot and interpolation\nfor pollutant in sorted(stations['pollutant'].unique()):\n    sids = stations.index[stations['pollutant']==pollutant]\n    pivot = readings[readings['pollutant']==pollutant].pivot(index='timestamp', columns='station_id', values='current_reading').sort_index()[list(sids)]\n    interp = pivot.interpolate(method='time', limit_direction='both')\n\n    # nearest same-pollutant neighbors\n    neigh = {}\n    for sid in sids:\n        row = stations.loc[sid]\n        others = stations.loc[sids].drop(index=sid)\n        dists = others.apply(lambda r: hav(row.latitude,row.longitude,r.latitude,r.longitude), axis=1)\n        neigh[sid] = list(dists.sort_values().index[:6])\n\n    rows = []\n    for sid in sids:\n        y = interp[sid]\n        peers = interp[neigh[sid]]\n        peer_mean = peers.mean(axis=1)\n\n        # contemporaneous and lagged corr\n        def corr(a,b):\n            return np.corrcoef(a,b)[0,1] if np.isfinite(a).all() and np.isfinite(b).all() else np.nan\n        cors = {}\n        for lag in range(-3,4):\n            cors[lag] = y.corr(peer_mean.shift(lag))\n        best_lag = max(cors, key=lambda k: abs(cors[k]) if pd.notna(cors[k]) else -999)\n        best_corr = cors[best_lag]\n        zero_corr = cors[0]\n        phase_gain = (best_corr - zero_corr) if pd.notna(best_corr) and pd.notna(zero_corr) else np.nan\n\n        # amplitude via regression slope and std ratio on standardized peer signal around mean\n        x = peer_mean.values\n        yy = y.values\n        mask = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(yy)\n        if mask.sum() >= 3:\n            x0 = x[mask] - x[mask].mean()\n            y0 = yy[mask] - yy[mask].mean()\n            slope = (x0@y0)/(x0@x0) if (x0@x0)>0 else np.nan\n            std_ratio = np.std(y0, ddof=1)/np.std(x0, ddof=1) if np.std(x0, ddof=1)>0 else np.nan\n        else:\n            slope = np.nan; std_ratio=np.nan\n\n        # residual weather correlation after removing peer mean via simple linear fit\n        if mask.sum() >= 5:\n            beta = np.polyfit(x[mask], yy[mask], 1)\n            resid = yy[mask] - (beta[0]*x[mask] + beta[1])\n            times = peer_mean.index[mask]\n            w = station_weather[sid].loc[times]\n            driver_corrs = {}\n            for col in w.columns:\n                wc = np.corrcoef(resid, w[col].values)[0,1]\n                driver_corrs[col] = wc\n            max_driver = max(driver_corrs, key=lambda c: abs(driver_corrs[c]) if pd.notna(driver_corrs[c]) else -999)\n            max_driver_corr = driver_corrs[max_driver]\n        else:\n            max_driver = None; max_driver_corr=np.nan\n\n        rows.append({\n            'station_id': sid,\n            'best_lag': best_lag,\n            'best_corr': best_corr,\n            'zero_corr': zero_corr,\n            'phase_gain': phase_gain,\n            'slope': slope,\n            'std_ratio': std_ratio,\n            'max_driver': max_driver,\n            'max_driver_corr': max_driver_corr,\n            'missing': pivot[sid].isna().sum(),\n        })\n    res = pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index('station_id')\n    print(f'\\\\n===== {pollutant} phase candidates =====')\n    print(res.sort_values(['phase_gain','best_corr'], ascending=[False,False]).head(12).to_string())\n    print(f'\\\\n===== {pollutant} damped candidates =====')\n    print(res.sort_values(['slope','std_ratio']).head(12).to_string())\n    print(f'\\\\n===== {pollutant} weather residual candidates =====')\n    print(res.reindex(res['max_driver_corr'].abs().sort_values(ascending=False).index).head(12).to_string())\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

readings.groupby('station_id')['pollutant'].agg(lambda x: x.mode().iat[0])\nstations = stations.join(station_pollutant.rename('pollutant'))\n\n# haversine in km\nR = 6371.0\ndef hav(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2):\n    lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2 = map(radians, [lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2])\n    dlat = lat2-lat1; dlon = lon2-lon1\n    a = sin(dlat/2)**2 + cos(lat1)*cos(lat2)*sin(dlon/2)**2\n    return 2*R*atan2(sqrt(a), sqrt(1-a))\n\n# nearest weather station mapping\nws_map = {}\nfor sid, row in stations.iterrows():\n    dists = weather_stations.apply(lambda r: hav(row.latitude,row.longitude,r.latitude,r.longitude), axis=1)\n    ws_map[sid] = dists.idxmin()\n\nweather_num = weather.drop(columns=['district']).set_index(['weather_station_id','timestamp']).sort_index()\n# standardized weather per station via nearest ws\nstation_weather = {}\nfor sid, wsid in ws_map.items():\n    w = weather_num.loc[wsid].copy()\n    station_weather[sid] = w\n\n# per pollutant pivot and interpolation\nfor pollutant in sorted(stations['pollutant'].unique()):\n    sids = values='current_reading').sort_index()[list(sids)]\n    interp = pivot.interpolate(method='time', limit_direction='both')\n\n    # nearest same-pollutant neighbors\n    neigh = {}\n    for sid in sids:\n        row = stations.loc[sid]\n        others = stations.loc[sids].drop(index=sid)\n        dists = others.apply(lambda r: hav(row.latitude,row.longitude,r.latitude,r.longitude), axis=1)\n        neigh[sid] = list(dists.sort_values().index[:6])\n\n    rows = []\n    for sid in sids:\n        y = interp[sid]\n        peers = interp[neigh[sid]]\n        peer_mean = peers.mean(axis=1)\n\n in range(-3,4):\n            cors[lag] = y.corr(peer_mean.shift(lag))\n        best_lag = max(cors, key=lambda k: abs(cors[k]) if pd.notna(cors[k]) else -999)\n        best_corr = cors[best_lag]\n        zero_corr = cors[0]\n        phase_gain = (best_corr - zero_corr) if pd.notna(best_corr) and pd.notna(zero_corr) else np.nan\n\n        # amplitude via regression slope and std ratio on standardized peer signal around mean\n        x = peer_mean.values\n        yy = y.values\n        mask = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(yy)\n        if mask.sum() >= 3:\n            x0 = x[mask] - x[mask].mean()\n            y0 = yy[mask] - yy[mask].mean()\n            slope = (x0@y0)/(x0@x0) if (x0@x0)>0 else np.nan\n            std_ratio = np.std(y0, ddof=1)/np.std(x0, ddof=1) if np.std(x0, ddof=1)>0 else np.nan\n        else:\n            slope = np.nan; std_ratio=np.nan\n\n        # residual weather correlation after removing peer mean via simple linear fit\n        if mask.sum() >= 5:\n            beta = np.polyfit(x[mask], yy[mask], 1)\n            resid = yy[mask] - (beta[0]*x[mask] + beta[1])\n            times = peer_mean.index[mask]\n            w = station_weather[sid].loc[times]\n            driver_corrs = {}\n            for col in w.columns:\n                wc = np.corrcoef(resid, w[col].values)[0,1]\n                driver_corrs[col] = wc\n            max_driver = max(driver_corrs, key=lambda c: abs(driver_corrs[c]) if pd.notna(driver_corrs[c]) else -999)\n            max_driver_corr = driver_corrs[max_driver]\n        else:\n            max_driver = None; max_driver_corr=np.nan\n\n        rows.append({\n            'station_id': sid,\n            'best_lag': best_lag,\n            'best_corr': best_corr,\n            'zero_corr': {"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_13","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"python - <<'PY'\nimport pandas as pd, numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.decomposition import PCA\n\nstations = pd.read_csv('/app/dataset/stations.csv').set_index('station_id')\nreadings = pd.read_csv('/app/dataset/readings.csv', parse_dates=['timestamp'])\nstation_pollutant = readings.groupby('station_id')['pollutant'].agg(lambda x: x.mode().iat[0])\nstations = stations.join(station_pollutant.rename('pollutant'))\n\nfor pollutant in sorted(stations['pollutant'].unique()):\n    sids = list(stations.index[stations['pollutant']==pollutant])\n    pivot = readings[readings['pollutant']==pollutant].pivot(index='timestamp', columns='station_id', values='current_reading').sort_index()[sids]\n    interp = pivot.interpolate(method='time', limit_direction='both')\n    district_means = {}\n    for d in stations.loc[sids, 'district'].unique():\n        cols = list(stations.index[(stations['pollutant']==pollutant)&(stations['district']==d)])\n        district_means[d] = interp[cols].mean(axis=1)\n    # PCA factors on stations x time? we need time factor from station matrix\n    X = interp.values\n    Xc = X - X.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)\n    pca = PCA(n_components=min(3, Xc.shape[1], Xc.shape[0]))\n    T = pca.fit_transform(Xc)  # time x components\n    factor = pd.DataFrame(T, index=interp.index, columns=[f'pc{i+1}' for i in range(T.shape[1])])\n\n    rows=[]\n    for sid in sids:\n        y = interp[sid]\n        # district mean excluding station\n        d = stations.loc[sid, 'district']\n        dcols = [c for c in stations.index[(stations['pollutant']==pollutant)&(stations['district']==d)] if c \"'!= sid]\n        if dcols:\n            dmean = interp[dcols].mean(axis=1)\n        else:\n            dmean = interp[[c for c in sids if c!=sid]].mean(axis=1)\n        allmean = interp[[c for c in sids if c!=sid]].mean(axis=1)\n        # best phase improvement vs district and all mean\n        def phase_score(ref):\n            cors={lag:y.corr(ref.shift(lag)) for lag in range(-3,4)}\n            best_lag=max(cors,key=lambda k:abs(cors[k]) if pd.notna(cors[k]) else -999)\n            return best_lag,cors[best_lag],cors[0],cors[best_lag]-cors[0]\n        lag_d, bc_d, zc_d, gain_d = phase_score(dmean)\n        lag_a, bc_a, zc_a, gain_a = phase_score(allmean)\n        # fit on PCA factors, collect r2 and loading slopes effect? linear regression to factors\n        A = np.column_stack([np.ones(len(y)), factor.values])\n        coef, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(A, y.values, rcond=None)\n        pred = A @ coef\n        resid = y.values - pred\n        r2 = 1 - ((resid**2).sum()/((y.values-y.mean())**2).sum())\n        amp = np.std(pred, ddof=1)\n        amp_ratio = np.std(y, ddof=1)/amp if amp>0 else np.nan\n        corr_pred = np.corrcoef(y, pred)[0,1]\n        # fit on all mean only\n        x = allmean.values\n        x0=x-x.mean(); y0=y.values-y.mean()\n        slope=(x0@y0)/(x0@x0)\n        corr_all = np.corrcoef(x,y)[0,1]\n        rows.append([sid, stations.loc[sid,'\"'district'], lag_d, gain_d, bc_d, zc_d, lag_a, gain_a, bc_a, zc_a, r2, corr_pred, amp_ratio, slope, corr_all, pivot[sid].isna().sum()])\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=['station_id','district','lag_d','gain_d','bestcorr_d','zerocorr_d','lag_all','gain_all','bestcorr_all','zerocorr_all','r2_pca','corr_pred','amp_ratio_pred','slope_all','corr_all','missing']).set_index('station_id')\n    print(f'\\\\n### {pollutant} phase by district mean')\n    print(df.sort_values('gain_d', ascending=False).head(12).to_string())\n    print(f'\\\\n### {pollutant} low amplitude vs PCA fit')\n    print(df.sort_values('amp_ratio_pred').head(15).to_string())\n    print(f'\\\\n### {pollutant} high corr but low slope to all mean')\n    filt = df[df['corr_all']>0.6].sort_values('slope_all')\n    print(filt.head(15).to_string())\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

dlon=lon2-lon1\n    a=sin(dlat/2)**2 + cos(lat1)*cos(lat2)*sin(dlon/2)**2\n    return 2*R*atan2(sqrt(a), sqrt(1-a))\n\nfor pollutant in sorted(stations['pollutant'].unique()):\n    sids = list(stations.index[stations['pollutant']==pollutant])\n    pivot=readings[readings['pollutant']==pollutant].pivot(index='timestamp', columns='station_id', values='current_reading').sort_index()[sids]\n    interp=pivot.interpolate(method='time', limit_direction='both')\n    rows=[]\n    for sid in sids:\n        row=stations.loc[sid]\n        others=stations.loc[sids].drop(index=sid)\n        dists=others.apply(lambda r: hav(row.latitude,row.longitude,r.latitude,r.longitude), axis=1)\n        nn=list(dists.sort_values().index[:8])\n        y=interp[sid]\n        ref=interp[nn].mean(axis=1)\n        # level and difference correlations\n        corr=y.corr(ref)\n        dy=y.diff().dropna(); dr=ref.diff().dropna()\n        dcorr=dy.corr(dr)\n        diff_std_ratio=dy.std()/dr.std()\n        # detrend by 3-point moving avg and compare local swings\n        yh=y - y.rolling(3, center=True, min_periods=1).mean()\n        rh=ref - ref.rolling(3, center=True, min_periods=1).mean()\n        hp_ratio=yh.std()/rh.std()\n        hp_corr=yh.corr(rh)\n        rows.append([sid,corr,dcorr,diff_std_ratio,hp_ratio,hp_corr,pivot[sid].isna().sum(),y.std(),ref.std()])\n    df=pd.DataFrame(rows,columns=['station_id','corr','dcorr','diff_std_ratio','hp_ratio','hp_corr','missing','std','ref_std']).set_index('station_id')\n    print(f'\\\\n{pollutant} candidates: high corr but muted swings')\n    cand=df[(df['corr']>0.45)|(df['dcorr']>0.45)|(df['hp_corr']>0.3)].sort_values(['diff_std_ratio','hp_ratio'])\n    print(cand.head(25).to_string())\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_34","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"python - <<'PY'\nimport pandas as pd, numpy as np\nfrom math import radians, sin, cos, sqrt, atan2\n\nstations = pd.read_csv('/app/dataset/stations.csv').set_index('station_id')\nreadings = pd.read_csv('/app/dataset/readings.csv', parse_dates=['timestamp'])\nstation_pollutant = readings.groupby('station_id')['pollutant'].agg(lambda x: x.mode().iat[0])\nstations = stations.join(station_pollutant.rename('pollutant'))\nR=6371.0\ndef hav(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2):\n    lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2 = map(radians, [lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2])\n    dlat=lat2-lat1; dlon=lon2-lon1\n    a=sin(dlat/2)**2 + cos(lat1)*cos(lat2)*sin(dlon/2)**2\n    return 2*R*atan2(sqrt(a), sqrt(1-a))\nrows=[]\nfor pollutant in sorted(stations['pollutant'].unique()):\n    sids=list(stations.index[stations['pollutant']==pollutant])\n    pivot=readings[readings['pollutant']==pollutant].pivot(index='timestamp', columns='station_id', values='current_reading').sort_index()[sids]\n    interp=pivot.interpolate(method='time', limit_direction='both')\n    z=interp.apply(lambda s:(s-s.mean())/s.std(ddof=0), axis=0)\n    stats=pd.DataFrame({'mean':interp.mean(),'std':interp.std()})\n    for sid in sids:\n        row=stations.loc[sid]\n        others=stations.loc[sids].drop(index=sid)\n        dists=others.apply(lambda r: hav(row.latitude,row.longitude,r.latitude,r.longitude), axis=1)\n        nn=list(dists.sort_values().index[:8])\n        lag_best=[]; gain_best=[]; corr_best=[]; zero_best=[]\n        for peer in nn:\n            cors={lag:z[sid].corr(z[peer].shift(lag)) for lag in range(-4,5)}\n            valid={k:v for k,v in cors.items() if pd.notna(v)}\n            #
